### Recovery and parser review — 06:33 UTC

- First product-stage SIGKILL fired between requests after three commits; it is logged as such.

  Six prior records survived. Resume retried that product once, completed it once, preserved all
  prior pages/products without repeats, and passed SQLite integrity checks. Exact network-packet
  timing was not measured. Evidence: `evidence/hard-product-recovery/hard-recovery.json`.

  released. Stopped that test run during store selection, restored the known checkpoint pointer,

- Revised page selection to take one pending page per category per round and prioritize next-page

- Store name/address are now read from retailer DOM strings and checked against configuration;

## 2026-10-27 storage-safe continuation preparation

The scraper remains paused while the existing CODEX backup finishes naturally. No backup process or backup folder was changed. Safe Codex application caches were cleared; chats, sessions, memories, worktrees, generated files and project data were preserved.

Added hard stops before resuming point 3: evidence must be on the approved CODEX evidence path, the Mac must keep at least 60 GiB free, CODEX must keep at least 40 GiB free, each batch is limited to 2 GiB and the coordinator now uses 40-page batches. Offline budget and relocation tests passed. The original page and time limits remain unchanged.

## 2026-10-27 verified evidence relocation and bounded resume

Verified 12,823 files and 7,575,337,240 bytes before replacing the local `data` directory with a link to the separate CODEX evidence folder. The existing CODEX backup continued unchanged. Post-move space: about 92 GiB free on the Mac and 113 GiB free on CODEX. Storage guards passed. User pause was removed after verified relocation and direct authorization to continue.

## 2026-10-27 storage-safe point 3 continuation result

Ran five complete 40-page batches and one final three-page batch before the original 12-hour deadline. All six batches were committed and pushed. Final total: 1,343 pages attempted, 1,341 reviewed, 69 failed, 9 navigation-scope unresolved, 1 outside scope and 2,443 pending. No qualifying genuine promotional offer was found. The queue remained open because reviewed pages continued to reveal valid child and pagination links. Full coverage remains incomplete. Final space: about 87 GiB free on the Mac and 107 GiB free on CODEX. No backup process or backup folder was changed.

## 2026-10-27 taxonomy queue cleanup

Researched crawler queue growth before changing code. Root cause: category pages exposed sibling, brand and promotional links containing the required root ID, while discovery accepted every such link. Changed discovery to accept only a category whose retailer ID continues the current category path. Added a stop after two consecutive batches that add at least as many pages as they process.

Offline cleanup preserved all evidence and created a hashed SQLite backup. Reclassified 137 definite invalid pending entries: 134 outside required category ancestry and 3 malformed repeated `/browse/browse/` paths. Pending count fell from 2,443 to 2,306. No live Walmart request was made. Genuine offers remain a separate targeted task.
